# SECTION F–G — ELAI exhaustive super feature list

Compiled from family aggregates F and G for the exhaustive super feature list. Self-contained: each plan unit carries Status, Anchors, Composes with, Superseded notes, and every distinct behavior atom with ID.

- `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/F-AGGREGATE.md`
- `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/G-AGGREGATE.md`

## System: F — VibeSec/SHARD wiring-enforcement sessions (F-0..F-13, F-2c) and wiring doctrine

Family F turns VibeSec/SHARD security proposals into hard wiring done-gates (W-1..W-10): fail-closed write gates, security-rule measurement, tool-filter control plane, test integrity, staleness, skill library, formal/CBR verify, symbolic conflict, dependency age, security monotonicity, and post-F extensions (darwinian admit, audit conflicts, SSB archive).

Status rule: deep ledgers and explicit SUPERSEDED/as-built corrections beat the summary orientation pass. Higher plan numbers win **target state** when no explicit SUPERSEDED marker exists. Losing claims appear as **Superseded:** bullets with SUP/atom anchors. Deep passes did not independently re-verify product code; status labels are source assertions.

Source (read-only): `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/F-AGGREGATE.md`.

- `F-002/DF002-F1-001`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=35) F-1’s terminal scope is a deterministic, synchronous, in-process `PreToolUse` compound gate over candidate write content. _(anchor: F1:L3-L5, L15-L17; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-002`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=36) Tier-1 contains exactly two active axes: code vulnerability and secret exposure. _(anchor: F1:L5, L45-L47; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-003`: `[E/D]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=37) Dependency-existence and MCP/skill scanning are excluded from Tier-1 and may not inherit its fail-closed advertising. _(anchor: F1:L5-L6, L59-L61; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-004`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=38) The code-vulnerability axis delegates `BLOCK` matches to `RuleEngine` over F-2a-authored `vibesec` rows in the always-included `security` domain. _(anchor: F1:L7-L8, L23-L24, L47; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-005`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=39) The secret axis scans candidate source/diff with a compiled, reviewable constant regex set in `vibegate_secrets.rs`. _(anchor: F1:L47-L48; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-006`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=40) Secret predicates include AWS access keys matching `AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}`. _(anchor: F1:L47; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-007`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=41) Secret predicates include RSA/EC/OpenSSH private-key PEM headers. _(anchor: F1:L47; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-008`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=42) Secret predicates include assignments of at least eight quoted characters to `password`, `secret`, `token`, or `api[_-]?key`-like names. _(anchor: F1:L47; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-009`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=43) Any active-axis `Block` makes the compound result blocking; no prompt-controlled branch may bypass it. _(anchor: F1:L47, L51, L101-L103; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-010`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=44) Regex/evaluator initialization failure must panic at startup rather than silently return clean at gate time. _(anchor: F1:L47, L102-L103; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-011`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=45) The per-write hot path must propagate `Result`/`Option` and contain no `.unwrap()`/`.expect()`; only provably valid literal regex compilation may use init-time `expect`. _(anchor: F1:L47, L103; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F1-012`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-1`, ledger-line=46) F-2a security seed rows are inserted with `domain="security"`, `mutable=0`, and `source="SEED"`, making them always evaluated and auto-CCR-unretirable. _(anchor: F1:L24, L49; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- `F-002/DF002-F3-002`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=187) The task’s minimal tool subset is committed and frozen before the first untrusted repo/web/skill/MCP byte is observed. _(anchor: F3:L3, L11-L13; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-003`: `[E/S]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=188) Existing `ToolCategory::ALL` contains 16 categories and `FsmState::check_tool_allowed` provides the category gate. _(anchor: F3:L19-L20; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- `F-002/DF002-F3-005`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=190) `CommittedToolSet` stores `task_id`, a `BTreeSet<ToolCategory>`, `committed_at_ms`, `frozen`, and `commit_hash`. _(anchor: F3:L51-L53; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-006`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=191) `commit_minimal_set` intersects requested categories with `ToolCategory::ALL`, freezes the result, sorts it, and BLAKE3-hashes the allow-set plus task ID. _(anchor: F3:L52-L54; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-007`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=192) Researcher commitment includes search, read-only fetch, and role-allowed grounding methods, but excludes filesystem write, Bash, and edit. _(anchor: F3:L58; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-008`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=193) `check_against_commitment` classifies each tool and denies an out-of-set tool when the commitment is frozen. _(anchor: F3:L54; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-009`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=194) A frozen set treats an unknown tool as denied, never as default-allowed. _(anchor: F3:L60, L113; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F3-010`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=195) Enforcement is pure set membership and cannot consult an LLM/model to override a denial. _(anchor: F3:L56, L111; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- `F-002/DF002-F5-017`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=253) DKG contradiction edges are closed bi-temporally/non-destructively when the broadcast is applied. _(anchor: F5:L64, L183; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-018`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=254) Pending broadcasts are drained after `ensure_dkg_or_rebuild` through the existing knowledge SSB sink. _(anchor: F5:L64, L93-L100; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-019`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=255) `flag_on_staleness_broadcast` transitions cited Cosmos artifacts through the existing `CosmosStatus::Stale` path and returns a count. _(anchor: F5:L66; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-020`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=256) No new Cosmos status or decay engine is introduced. _(anchor: F5:L28, L66; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-021`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=257) `QuarantineBroadcastQueue` is an in-memory `tokio::sync::mpsc` with named capacity `QUARANTINE_QUEUE_CAPACITY=256`. _(anchor: F5:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-022`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=258) When full, the queue drops the oldest item and places a visible `dropped_count` on the next broadcast; silent loss is forbidden. _(anchor: F5:L68, L108, L157; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-023`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=259) The cascade is enabled by default; `ELAI_STALENESS_CASCADE_KILL=1` is disable-only. _(anchor: F5:L74, L103-L109, L153; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-024`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=260) Quarantine decisions are deterministic and cannot be overridden by an LLM/prompt. _(anchor: F5:L107; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-025`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=261) `get_staleness_quarantine` returns typed broadcast rows and aggregate DKG/Cosmos counts to a knowledge/staleness drawer. _(anchor: F5:L70, L86, L110; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-026`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=262) The Tauri command is registered in the invoke handler and the drawer invokes it through `@tauri-apps/api/core`. _(anchor: F5:L70; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-027`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=263) `elai dkg staleness-tick --workspace .` forces one production decay tick, drains the queue, applies both consumers, and prints a summary. _(anchor: F5:L72, L112-L114; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-028`: `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=264) The live trigger must journal `staleness_quarantine_broadcast` and either write `F-5-staleness-tick-*.json` or print `dependents_demoted`, with at least one DKG row changed to 2. _(anchor: F5:L111-L114, L152-L154; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-029`: `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=265) Mock-clock acceptance requires every citing concept to flip within one tick and the next grounding query to miss cache. _(anchor: F5:L156, L181; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-030`: `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=266) Queue-overflow acceptance requires visible dropped count on the next broadcast. _(anchor: F5:L157, L182; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-031`: `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=267) Completion requires executed wiring check, default-on proof, dashboard observation, golden/schema checks, cross-language parity, workspace compile, and auditor pass. _(anchor: F5:L150-L162; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-032`: `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=268) F-5 is fully deterministic/headless and has no RunPod/live-model split. _(anchor: F5:L162, L171, L229; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-033`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=269) The cascade uses SQLite updates and an in-process bounded queue; no network, subprocess, or external dependency is added. _(anchor: F5:L161, L176, L229; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-034`: `[E/D]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=270) Rules-crate 90-day decay is an explicitly excluded third isolated decay in v1. _(anchor: F5:L30; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-035`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=271) Phase 2 is blocked until B-30 stabilizes `dkg_nodes`; Phase 1 can proceed independently. _(anchor: F5:L5, L27, L225; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-036`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=272) The stated `>1.0` flag and `>2.0` quarantine thresholds originate in SHARD; adopting `>2.0` for ELAI remains a decision to confirm. _(anchor: F5:L11, L220; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-037`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=273) Grounding-cache invalidation is only an assumed measurable until Phase 1 proves cache keys consume freshness. _(anchor: F5:L220; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-038`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=274) Drop-oldest may discard the most stale/important quarantine; visible loss and later capacity tuning are the only stated mitigations. _(anchor: F5:L226; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-039`: `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=275) The existing Cosmos status must be verified to contain a usable `Stale` transition before implementation. _(anchor: F5:L228; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-040`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=276) F-5’s decision record must preserve that ELAI already has bi-temporal memory semantics and that SHARD supplies a blueprint, not adopted code. _(anchor: F5:L74, L213-L217; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-041`: `[E/D]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=277) Future composition may demote skills that cite quarantined facts and feed F-7 utility, but neither is part of F-5. _(anchor: F5:L231-L233; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F5-042`: `[E/S]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=278) The plan asserts each broadcast row flows through the OTLP exporter for off-machine archival. _(anchor: F5:L233; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-01`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=201) Stale facts enter a SHARD-style time-decay quarantine. _(anchor: L96-L98; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-02`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=202) The self-healing cascade invalidates a stale fact. _(anchor: L96-L98; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-03`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=203) The cascade demotes dependent data. _(anchor: L96-L98; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-04`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=204) The cascade broadcasts the demotion across subsystems. _(anchor: L96-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-05`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=205) Invalidation is bi-temporal and non-destructive in the Graphiti style. _(anchor: L96-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-06`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=206) Mem0 A.U.D.N. is an input pattern for the design. _(anchor: L96-L98; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-07`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=207) E-5 already covers bi-temporal supersession only for `memory_facts`; F-5 expands the scope. _(anchor: L98-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-08`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=208) A quarantined fact’s demotion propagates to dependent DKG concept annotations. _(anchor: L99-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-09`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=209) A quarantined fact’s demotion propagates to `grounding_cache`. _(anchor: L99-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-10`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=210) A quarantined fact’s demotion propagates to Cosmos artifacts. _(anchor: L99-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-11`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=211) One broadcast is the cross-subsystem propagation mechanism. _(anchor: L99-L100; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-12`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=212) Existing `concept_stale` is asserted to be always `1` and never populated. _(anchor: L99-L101; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-13`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=213) Before population, Phase 1 audits every `concept_stale` consumer. _(anchor: L101-L104; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-14`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=214) Before population, Phase 1 adds a retrieval contract test with `concept_stale=0`. _(anchor: L101-L104; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-15`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=215) The consumer audit and contract test occur after B-30. _(anchor: L102-L104; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-16`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=216) Phase 2 implements the cascade through a bounded fan-out queue. _(anchor: L104-L105; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-17`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=217) The fan-out channel has an explicit capacity. _(anchor: L104-L105; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_
- `SUMMARY/F5-18`: `[E]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=218) Bounded fan-out prevents scheduler-tick pile-up. _(anchor: L104-L105; source: 06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md)_

- **Status:** planned (extends shipped TemplateCrystallizer)

- **Composes with:**
  - `F-002/DF002-I-007` → `[I]``[I]` (unit=`I`, ledger-line=475) F-6’s S4 rule makes human approval happen at promotion time, while sub-S4 execution routes to a human gate; after promotion, S4 execution is autonomous without a per-invocation approval. _(anchor: F6:L139-L144, L161-L170; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
  - `F-002/DF002-I-008` → `[I]``[I]` (unit=`I`, ledger-line=476) F-6’s reuse of an SSB verdict assumes the SSB row and crystallization outcome share a stable correlation key and persistence boundary; otherwise replay/restart can alter acquisition results. _(anchor: F6:L189-L200, L473-L477; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **Superseded:** `SUP-010 — F-6 acquisition sequence and live trigger` Deep: curriculum sequence is Voyager context; acquisition begins at crystallization; live observable is library promote+tick, not S2 autonomous dispatch. **Target state (winner):** F-002/DF002-SD-037, DF002-SD-040 **Superseded / losing description:** SUMMARY/F6-01..F6-05 curriculum→generation→execution→retry; F6-30 S2 autonomous dispatch live trigger **Anchors:** F6:L40-L49,L296-L299 vs SUM:L114-L125
- **Superseded:** `SUP-011 — F-6 skill event naming` Deep: Kind values under one skill_library_event Pattern-2 variant. **Target state (winner):** F-002/DF002-SD-039 **Superseded / losing description:** SUMMARY/F6-32,F6-33 skill_trust_promoted / skill_demoted_stale as independent action types **Anchors:** F6:L148-L159 vs SUM:L124-L125
- `F-002/DF002-F6-001`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=286) A learned skill is acquired only through a critic gate, governed by a separate execution-autonomy ladder, retrieved through the existing reranker, and demoted on staleness. _(anchor: F6:L1-L9, L38-L49; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F6-002`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=287) Delivery is ordered C6a→C6b→C6c, each gated on its predecessor. _(anchor: F6:L11-L14, L125, L187, L216; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F6-003`: `[E/S]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=288) `TemplateCrystallizer` is asserted shipped and crystallizes after `CRYSTALLIZATION_THRESHOLD_N` consecutive successes. _(anchor: F6:L16-L18, L68-L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F6-004`: `[E/S]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=289) BestOfN already invokes `MiniCritic` and journals Pass/Warn/Block verdicts; F-6 may not make a fresh critic call. _(anchor: F6:L22-L24, L75-L79; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- `F-002/DF002-F6-007`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=292) No `From`/`Into` conversion may exist between skill and memory trust types. _(anchor: F6:L130-L131, L291; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- `F-002/DF002-F6-008`: `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=293) `LearnedSkill` stores UUID, concept class, plan-skeleton hash, tier, invocation/failure counts, acquired/last-verified times, optional embedding reference, and optional critic-verdict SSB ID. _(anchor: F6:L132-L136; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- `F-001/DF001-F9-E001`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=170) F-9 extends a real PyPI/npm existence-and-age checker from PR time to Edit/Write PreToolUse time, adds crates.io, per-ecosystem thresholds, a local cache, live block telemetry, CLI, and dashboard. _(anchor: L1-L5; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E002`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=171) D-11 is explicitly PLANNED/not shipped and is a hard blocker; E-41/E-11 are shipped composition surfaces and B-19 latency budgets are planned. _(anchor: L7-L13; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E003`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=172) The source corrects “shape-only” to a real HTTP checker and narrows the true gaps to invocation timing, cargo coverage, and a hard-coded 730-day age threshold. _(anchor: L17-L25; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E004`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=173) Network lookup is explicitly not a fail-closed hot-path guarantee: cache hits are in-process; misses are Tier-2 best effort and may fail open under registry unavailability. _(anchor: L27-L29; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E005`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=174) Socket Firewall is defense in depth at install time but is moved to an operations runbook and contributes no plan code. _(anchor: L29; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E006`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=175) Existing E-41 dep parsing is a cheap deterministic PR prefilter and lacks Cargo section awareness; F-9 preserves it while adding registry-grounded write-time enforcement. _(anchor: L31-L39; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E007`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=176) _check_cargo calls crates.io through the existing HTTP helper, derives age from updated_at/newest created_at, keeps the existing tuple shape, accepts ecosystem in stdin JSON, and maps network failure to inapplicable. _(anchor: L54-L57; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E008`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=177) Default maximum ages become PyPI 730, npm 730, and cargo 1825 days; an explicit claim overrides the default, and 1825 is a tunable unverified design choice. _(anchor: L58; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E009`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=178) Cargo parsing becomes section-aware for dependencies, dev-dependencies, and build-dependencies and returns DepCandidate{name, Ecosystem} for Pypi/Npm/Cargo while keeping the PR leg compatible. _(anchor: L60; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E010`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=179) DepWriteTimeGate fires only for Edit/Write dependency content/path patterns and returns immediately with no finding on other writes. _(anchor: L62-L64; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E011`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=180) LruExistenceCache is a bounded HashMap plus VecDeque with about a one-hour TTL and no new lru dependency. _(anchor: L64-L66; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E012`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=181) A known-good hit allows without network; a miss invokes RegistryCheck; NotFound or TooNew blocks and emits write_time_slopsquat_blocked; Ok populates the cache. _(anchor: L64-L65; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F9-E013`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=182) RegistryUnreachable allows the write but must emit an advisory row; it may never silently pass. _(anchor: L65, L109-L115; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

- `F-001/DF001-F9-E015`: `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=184) WriteTimeSlopsquatBlockedPayload stores a BLAKE3 dep-name hash, ecosystem, NotFound/TooNew/RegistryUnreachable kind, optional registry age, and timestamp in one baseline-plus-one action. _(anchor: L70; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

- `F-001/DF001-X-I002`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=449) F-8 symbolic review, F-10 CWE monotonicity, and F-11’s four-guard chain are distinct self-mod axes, but the sources do not define one canonical order or transaction across all three. _(anchor: F-8 L61-L71, L238-L240; F-10 L56, L90; F-11 L64-L80, L254-L256; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I003`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=450) F-9 is the write-time registry leg, E-41 the PR prefilter, and Socket the install-time operational leg; F-1 may later fan the same dependency evaluator into VibeGate. _(anchor: F-9 L29-L39, L232-L238; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I004`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=451) F-12 explicitly orders exact-key audit, age quarantine, and semantic adjudication on one memory tick, but concurrent facts can change between those passes without a stated snapshot transaction. _(anchor: F-12 L72-L75, L107-L124; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I005`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=452) Every new SSB action increases the chance that F-13’s migration trigger runs on an old database; therefore archive behavior is operationally coupled to F-4/F-8/F-9/F-10/F-11 schema bumps even though no plan names that interaction. _(anchor: F-0 L136-L149, L223-L224; F-13 L185-L203; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I006`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=453) F-4’s additive None default means legacy PASS rows could become false fabrication evidence if queried without a “written under new schema” discriminator. _(anchor: F-4 L49-L55, L154; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I007`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=454) F-9’s advisory-on-unreachable keeps availability but cannot satisfy “zero hallucinated dependencies reach disk” during outages; the live 50/50 claim is necessarily conditioned on registry availability or a fixture. _(anchor: F-9 L65, L161-L170, L226-L231; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I008`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=455) F-10’s reconciled as-built section supports “partially implemented/wired” more strongly than the orientation summary’s “classifier does not exist,” but unchecked acceptance and a dark self-mod leg prevent a source-only DONE conclusion. _(anchor: F-10 L64-L106, L142-L154; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I009`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=456) F-11’s dual gate is a permission boundary for autonomous mutation, not merely configuration; its dry-run fallback is the only stated way to reconcile discoverability with least authority. _(anchor: F-11 L72, L177-L180, L243-L244; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I010`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=457) F-12’s local model minimizes fact-text disclosure, but the dashboard/SSB identifier trail still creates a privacy and retention surface not covered by “no cloud judge.” _(anchor: F-12 L68-L77, L253-L260; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-X-I011`: `[I]``[IX]` (unit=`F-13`, ledger-line=458) F-13 archives the very audit database that can contain security/test/memory events, so filesystem permissions and deletion policy are part of the effective security contract even though absent from the plan. _(anchor: F-13 L17-L22, L285-L288, L497-L499; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

### User-facing surfaces (F)

Every window, panel, drawer, command, CLI verb, flag, status display, or notification named by a SURFACE-tagged atom or surfaces inventory row. One line each with atom ID. Deduplicated by atom ID (first occurrence wins).

#### Surfaces — F-0
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E006`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=46) W-1 makes python .claude/tools/wiring_contract_check.py --bn-file <F-N>.md --run-trigger returning exit 0 a mandatory completion gate. _(anchor: L47-L55; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E012`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=52) W-3 requires reachability from a real CLI/slash command, FSM transition/evidence gate, PreToolUse/PostToolUse hook, or Tauri command; test-only callers are wire-dark. _(anchor: L64-L68; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E013`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=53) W-4 requires every human-visible SSB action or signal to name a Svelte/Tauri frontend pair and pass cross_lang_diff; only explicitly justified pure-internal gates are exempt. _(anchor: L70-L75; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E014`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=54) W-5 defines observable_effect as a human-runnable command or dashboard action that produces both a live ssb.db row and an inspectable file/stdout artifact; a unit test is not sufficient. _(anchor: L77-L83; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E016`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=56) W-6 requires at least one dashboard-verified acceptance item, with a one-line N/A justification only for truly headless work. _(anchor: L85-L88; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E021`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=61) Global completion requires cargo check, sole-auditor PASS, wiring trigger PASS, cross-language PASS when applicable, dashboard E2E, and a memory-writer run. _(anchor: L112-L115; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E033`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=73) The schema registry reserves F-1 VibeGateBlocked kinds for code, secret, dependency, and skill axes, with only code/secret in Tier 1. _(anchor: L136-L141; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E040`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=80) The headline move is F-1 Tier 1 plus F-2a into F-2b, producing an auditable and reproducible pre-write before/after insecure-rate grounded by SSB and ELAI_SEED. _(anchor: L165-L170; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E042`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=82) Every dependency must be verified for name, version, and last-published date; a new external dependency requires user confirmation, and CLI subprocesses require Tier-C isolation. _(anchor: L178-L181; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E044`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=84) The session log begins empty and, on shipment, records context compiler, integration explorer, spec grounder, executor, auditor/fixer, wiring validator, dashboard smoker, memory writer, and committing. _(anchor: L187-L192; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E047`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=87) F-11 closes one config-only DGM generation, explicitly calls the otherwise-bypassed security filter, remains behind darwinian-evolve plus ELAI_DARWINIAN_LOOP_ENABLE, and adds darwinian_admit. _(anchor: L205-L211; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- `F-001/DF001-F0-E051`: `[E]` (unit=`F-0`, ledger-line=91) The prescribed next step is the Tier-1 F-2a/F-1 headline move followed by F-2b when a 2x3090 RunPod window exists, always under W-1..W-10. _(anchor: L226-L233; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

### Audit (F)
- **Units covered:** 18 — F-0, F-global, F-1, F-2, F-2c, F-3, F-4, F-5, F-6, F-7, F-8, F-9, F-10, F-11, F-12, F-13, F-WD, F-registers
- **Unit entry count:** 18
- **Behavior bullets (atoms under units):** 1308
- **User-facing surfaces count:** 315
- **Superseded notes inlined:** 20
- **Unique atom IDs in family parse:** 1308
- **IX/DEP edges collected:** 24
- **Source aggregate:** `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/F-AGGREGATE.md`
- **Policy:** formatting transform only; every distinct atom ID from deep ledgers + summary inventory (plus status/inv/dep/IX/SD/AC/INF/SUP registers) emitted with ID; surfaces inventory deduped by ID; losing descriptions retained as Superseded bullets under newer-plan precedence.

## System: G — Trust READ/EVIDENCE/RUNTIME surfaces (taint, RRA, blast radius, MCP re-pin, cockpit, SAFEDELTA, confidence, attestation, redaction, model-drift)

Family G owns the trust **read / evidence / runtime** layer that composes F verdicts rather than restating them: data-plane taint, reproducible-run artifacts, blast-radius FS/egress gates, MCP rug-pull re-pin, reviewer cockpit, SAFEDELTA benchmarks, claim confidence + lethal trifecta, in-toto attestation, secret redaction, and model-drift canaries.

Status rule: deep ledgers and explicit SUPERSEDED/as-built corrections beat the summary orientation pass. Higher plan numbers win **target state** when no explicit SUPERSEDED marker exists. Losing claims appear as **Superseded:** bullets with SUP/atom anchors. Deep passes did not independently re-verify product code; status labels are source assertions.

Source (read-only): `docs/thefinalglance/feature-discovery/aggregates/G-AGGREGATE.md`.

- `G-001/DG001-INF-005`: [I] basis=`The declared payload has aggregate counts, while per-hunk line provenance remains underspecified.`: G-5’s aggregate result row can reproduce the DSR division but cannot alone re-derive which lines/hunks were vouched.
- `G-001/DG001-INF-006`: [I] basis=`Cross-source schedule supports the ordering; implementation availability is not independently verified.`: G-7’s newly explicit G-9 secret-detector bridge is sequence-compatible because G-9 is Wave 2 and G-7 Wave 3, but it is still a missing declared prerequisite.
- `G-002/DG002-I-001`: [I] basis=`G-2 is the keystone while both G-2 and G-9 call G-9 an export precondition; exact release sequencing is not specified.`: Delivery can implement G-2’s local artifact before G-9, but any external RRA release should wait for G-9.
- `G-002/DG002-I-002`: [I] basis=`G6:L126-L127; G2:L74-L90; G8:L304-L308. Signing composition is not directly wired in G-6.`: The intended signed SAFEDELTA manifest likely means a G-2 RRA plus G-8 attestation, because G-2 itself hashes but does not define a signature.
- `G-002/DG002-I-003`: [I] basis=`Their schemas share seed/model/fingerprint/hash values, but no cross-artifact canonicalization contract is stated.`: G-2 roots, G-8 predicates, and G-10 baselines need a shared canonical identity/serialization convention to compare across machines.
- `G-002/DG002-I-004`: [I] basis=`The sources say redaction is pre-persistence and export-safe, but run-exit ordering is not explicitly diagrammed.`: G-9 should run before G-2/G-8 serialization at run exit, otherwise already-collected raw values could enter exported material despite later sink redaction.
- `G-002/DG002-I-005`: [I] basis=`Two trigger classes and one-row language are explicit; locking/deduplication is absent.`: Concurrent scheduled and run-end G-10 checks need coalescing/idempotency to avoid duplicate drift rows and canary cost.
- `G-002/DG002-I-006`: [I] basis=`Core redaction is default-on and advisory wire-dark; the panel promises counts but the counter store is not fixed.`: The optional G-9 advisory cannot be the sole source for dashboard counts because it may be disabled while redaction remains on.
- `G-002/DG002-I-007`: [I] basis=``identity_key` includes model/provider/lane, while the signal name is narrower; source does not resolve mapping.`: A G-10 `FingerprintChanged` signal should probably be reserved for actual fingerprint change, not every `identity_key` change.
- `G-002/DG002-I-008`: [I] basis=`The source uses “signed” and `sign_local_merkle` but defines no secret/private key or external trust root.`: A local Merkle tree proves integrity only relative to a trusted root; calling it “signed” likely requires an unstated trust/key binding.

- `G-001/DG001-G1-E002`: [E] area=`Existing capability/status` anchor=`G1:L5-L16, L48-L54`: Reuse shipped `Provenance`, `Provenanced<T>`, `from_web`, `from_tool_output`, `from_file`, `from_sandbox`, `allow_tool_with_origin`, and `DESTRUCTIVE_TOOLS`; constructors are test-only/dead-wired at the stated baseline. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E002)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E003`: [E] area=`Core workflow/security` anchor=`G1:L9-L16, L31-L36`: Label every ingest byte, propagate only causally relevant provenance, and deterministically gate an Untrusted context before a destructive sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E003)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E004`: [E] area=`Boundary/dependency` anchor=`G1:L12-L14, L18-L21, L56-L62`: G-1 is data-plane defense across tool-capable agents; F-3 remains the control-plane minimal-tool-set commitment using the Researcher role envelope. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E004)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E005`: [E] area=`Integration/data flow` anchor=`G1:L18-L21, L59-L60`: E-37 `classify_injection`/`ClassifierVerdict` seeds an Untrusted label on a malicious span. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E005)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E006`: [E] area=`Constraint/tiering` anchor=`G1:L15-L16`: Gate-time work is wholly W-10 Tier 1: no network, subprocess, or RunPod; estimated two sessions/eight-to-twelve hours. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E006)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E007`: [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G1:L22-L24`: G-1 is parallel-safe with G-4 and G-2, but not concurrent with edits to safety provenance/capability modules or the live dispatch fan-in. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E007)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E008`: [E] area=`Security model` anchor=`G1:L38-L46`: The intended information-flow label is a four-point integrity × confidentiality lattice, with P-T blocking Untrusted integrity and P-F restricting confidentiality egress. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E008)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E009`: [E] area=`Determinism/precision` anchor=`G1:L38-L44`: The dependency-screened join is mandatory; joining every context region is forbidden because unrelated content would over-taint the task. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E009)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E010`: [E] area=`Existing API/schema` anchor=`G1:L48-L53`: Existing `Provenance` surfaces also include `from_memory`, `user_prompt`, `system`, `is_trusted`, `is_untrusted`, `label_blake3_prefix`; `Provenanced<T>` exposes `new/trusted/value/into_value/provenance/is_untrusted/map`; types include `TrustLevel` and `TextSource`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E010)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-C001`: [C] area=`Corrected integration` anchor=`G1:L63-L70`: Dated correction: `agent_pool.rs` is a sandbox subprocess launcher with no ingest/sink; real ingest is `web_research_orchestrator.rs:434`, `mcp_tool_bridge.rs:298`, `codeact_sandbox.rs:251`, and `production_agent_rpc.rs:1503`, while `production_agent_rpc.rs::translate_tool_request -> enforce_taint_sink_gate` is the live fan/sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-C001)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E011`: [E] area=`API/algorithm` anchor=`G1:L85-L94`: `join_screened(relevant: &[&Provenance]) -> Provenance` computes the element-wise most restrictive join, with Untrusted dominating Trusted, over only dependency-relevant provenance. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E011)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E012`: [E] area=`State/schema` anchor=`G1:L87-L92`: `TaintedContext` carries the joined provenance keyed by task/turn ID. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E012)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E013`: [E] area=`API/integration` anchor=`G1:L91-L94`: `seed_from_verdict(&ClassifierVerdict) -> Provenance` bridges E-37 and forces Untrusted for a malicious verdict. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E013)_
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E014`: [E] area=`Data-flow constraint` anchor=`G1:L92-L94, L294-L295`: The relevant set is defined initially as bytes actually read by the in-flight tool call; it must not be all context. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E014)_

- `G-001/DG001-G1-E016`: [E] area=`Ingest mapping` anchor=`G1:L96-L100`: WebFetch/WebSearch bodies use `Provenance::from_web(url)` and are Untrusted. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E016)_

- `G-001/DG001-G1-E018`: [E] area=`Ingest mapping/security` anchor=`G1:L96-L100`: File reads use `from_file(path)`; committed `.elai_cc/` source is Trusted and other files are Untrusted. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E018)_

### G-3: Deterministic runtime blast-radius gates (FS + egress IP pin)
- **Status:** planned (header) / partial as-built — status conflicted
- **Anchors:** `G-001/DG001-G3-E001` @ G3:L1-L3; `G-001/DG001-G3-E002` @ G3:L5-L13; `G-001/DG001-G3-E003` @ G3:L5-L10; `G-001/DG001-G3-E004` @ G3:L9-L10; `G-001/DG001-G3-E005` @ G3:L11-L15, L77-L83; `G-001/DG001-G3-E006` @ G3:L13-L15; `G-001/DG001-G3-E007` @ G3:L15-L16; `G-001/DG001-G3-E008` @ G3:L18-L24; `G-001/DG001-G3-E009` @ G3:L25-L27; `G-001/DG001-G3-E010` @ G3:L30-L37; `G-001/DG001-G3-E011` @ G3:L38-L42, L54-L59; `G-001/DG001-G3-E012` @ G3:L54-L57; `G-001/DG001-G3-E013` @ G3:L54-L58; `G-001/DG001-G3-E014` @ G3:L59-L63; `G-001/DG001-G3-E015` @ G3:L71-L73; `G-001/DG001-G3-E016` @ G3:L73-L75; `G-001/DG001-G3-E017` @ G3:L74-L75; `G-001/DG001-G3-E018` @ G3:L108-L117; `G-001/DG001-G3-E019` @ G3:L108-L113; `G-001/DG001-G3-E020` @ G3:L112-L113; `G-001/DG001-G3-E021` @ G3:L113-L115; `G-001/DG001-G3-E022` @ G3:L115-L116; `G-001/DG001-G3-E023` @ G3:L116-L117, L174-L181; `G-001/DG001-G3-E024` @ G3:L119-L125; … +64 more atom IDs under this unit
- **Composes with:**
  - `G-001/DG001-XINT-003` → [E] [interaction] anchor=`G3:L106-L150, L170-L192, L301-L305`: Blast radius: E-30 approves/rejects raw verb → G-3 obtains expanded target/host → canonical/path or resolved-IP membership check → fail-closed `HookFinding` → `blast_radius_blocked` with both command forms → drawer/RRA/cockpit.
  - `G-001/DG001-XINT-008` → [E] [interaction] anchor=`G0:L143-L156`: Sequencing: Wave 1 G-4+G-1 → G-1 unlocks G-3/G-7 → Wave 2 begins with G-2 and includes G-3/G-5/G-8/G-9 → Wave 3 G-7/G-6; G-6 remains gated by live F-2b.
  - `SUMMARY/WF-G-03` → [E] [interaction] name=`G-3 filesystem gate`: shell input is captured -> tilde/glob/symlink expansion is canonicalized -> resolved target is checked against worktree -> operation fails closed before the verb if outside -> both command forms land in `blast_radius_blocked`. (GSI:L59-L75)
  - `SUMMARY/WF-G-04` → [E] [interaction] name=`G-3 egress gate`: hostname is checked for null/control bytes -> allowed host is resolved -> its IP is pinned host-side -> connect is blocked before dispatch on violation -> `blast_radius_blocked` records the forensic command context. (GSI:L61-L75)

- `G-001/DG001-G3-E002`: [E] area=`Capability/security` anchor=`G3:L5-L13`: Gate A validates expanded filesystem write/delete targets against the per-agent worktree before the verb executes. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E002)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E003`: [E] area=`Capability/security` anchor=`G3:L5-L10`: Gate B rejects null/control-character hosts and requires resolved IP membership in a pinned allow-set before connect. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E003)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E004`: [E] area=`Observability/forensics` anchor=`G3:L9-L10`: Both gates emit one `blast_radius_blocked` row with full pre- and post-expansion command forms. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E004)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E005`: [E] area=`Architecture/non-goal` anchor=`G3:L11-L15, L77-L83`: E-30 owns raw, whitespace-split, pre-expansion verb policy; G-3 owns expanded path/host policy and does not reimplement E-30. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E005)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E006`: [E] area=`Determinism/security` anchor=`G3:L13-L15`: Decisions are pure set/prefix membership and never delegated to an LLM permission classifier. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E006)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E007`: [E] area=`Constraint/tiering` anchor=`G3:L15-L16`: Gate-time work is wholly Tier 1 with no subprocess or RunPod; estimated one-to-two sessions/eight-to-twelve hours. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E007)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E008`: [E] area=`Existing capabilities` anchor=`G3:L18-L24`: Required shipped anchors are E-30 `check_argv`, `AllowlistVerdict`, `DENY_PATTERNS`, `argv_blake3`, live `dispatch_pre_tool_use`, Linux `allowed_hosts`/iptables setup, and post-G-1 provenance/capability surfaces. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E008)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E009`: [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G3:L25-L27`: Work is parallel-safe with G-2/G-4 but not with concurrent edits to the PreToolUse dispatcher, sandbox networking modules, or SSB write path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E009)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E010`: [E] area=`Gap/forensics` anchor=`G3:L30-L37`: E-30’s current raw split misses `$HOME`, glob, tilde, and symlink expansion and does not log what the shell ultimately expands. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E010)_
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E011`: [E] area=`Existing state/security` anchor=`G3:L38-L42, L54-L59`: Linux networking currently resolves/pins IPs via veth+iptables OUTPUT while seccomp opens AF_INET/AF_INET6 STREAM/DGRAM and explicitly does not filter egress. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E011)_

- `G-001/DG001-G5-E005`: [E] area=`Determinism` anchor=`G5:L8-L14`: DSR must be recomputed deterministically/byte-identically from the SSB journal under GD-2. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E005)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E006`: [E] area=`Signal policy/default` anchor=`G5:L9-L11`: Findings are silent by default and surface only for a deterministic gate verdict or failing test, never model-only opinion. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E006)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E007`: [E] area=`Data gap/claim boundary` anchor=`G5:L11-L14, L40-L47`: The current journal has per-hunk verdict identity but no changed-line-count denominator, so G-5 must persist that denominator and may not claim preexisting auditability. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E007)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E008`: [E] area=`Deferred/non-goal` anchor=`G5:L14-L15`: The within-subjects human review study is separate measurement work, not the implementation done-gate. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E008)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E009`: [E] area=`Dependencies/evidence` anchor=`G5:L17-L20`: Existing sources are `TestOutcomePayload.stdout_hash`, `VibeGateBlockedPayload`/`SecurityRuleBlockedPayload` CWE, `GroundingSweepResultPayload`, and `ChangeDecisionPayload.hunk_id`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E009)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E010`: [E] area=`Existing surface/status` anchor=`G5:L20-L24`: Existing UI is `ReviewWindow.svelte -> get_file_changes -> SsbViewer::file_changes`; G-2 `rra.rs` was absent at the authoring baseline. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E010)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E011`: [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G5:L23-L24`: G-5 is parallel-safe with G-4/G-8/G-9, not with G-2 or concurrent changes to FileWrite/SSB write path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E011)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E012`: [E] area=`Product rationale/constraint` anchor=`G5:L27-L38`: Review-time evidence is deliberately deterministic because naive LLM review is treated as noisy/style-biased and weak on SQLi/XSS. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E012)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E013`: [E] area=`Data schema` anchor=`G5:L40-L42`: `ChangeDecisionPayload.hunk_id` is derived from a unified-diff `@@ -N,M +P,Q @@` header hash and provides the hunk-level precedent/numerator join. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E013)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E014`: [E] area=`Data status` anchor=`G5:L42-L46`: Existing `FileWritePayload` is only `{path,size_bytes,hash}` and `FileChange` is only `{path,action,summary}`; neither persists changed-line count. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E014)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E015`: [E] area=`Integration gap` anchor=`G5:L49-L52`: Existing dashboard access projects `has_stdout_hash` but lacks a dedicated `SsbViewer` TestOutcome reader. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E015)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E016`: [E] area=`CLI integration` anchor=`G5:L60-L63`: Authored CLI attach point is hand-rolled `args.rs::parse_args`/subcommand matching, mirroring the `run` parser arm. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E016)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E017`: [E] area=`Schema status` anchor=`G5:L62-L63`: Authoring baseline is schema 205 / variants 482. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E017)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E018`: [E] area=`Data schema` anchor=`G5:L91-L99`: `HunkVouch` is `{ hunk_id: String, vouched_by: Vec<VouchKind>, lines_changed: u32, needs_attention: bool }`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E018)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E019`: [E] area=`Data schema` anchor=`G5:L93-L96`: `VouchKind` is `TestStdoutHash`, `WriteGateVerdict { cwe: Option<String> }`, or `GroundingSweep`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E019)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E020`: [E] area=`API/algorithm` anchor=`G5:L93-L99`: `score_review_surface(file_changes, test_rows, gate_rows, sweep_rows) -> ReviewSurfaceScore` joins SSB evidence by hunk and divides unvouched-line sum by all changed-line sum. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E020)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E021`: [E] area=`Fallback/reliability` anchor=`G5:L97-L99`: A hunk without a vouch is conservatively `needs_attention=true`; no production `.unwrap()`/`.expect()`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E021)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E022`: [E] area=`Signal workflow/non-goal` anchor=`G5:L101-L104`: A surfaced finding requires VibeGate/SecurityRule CWE evidence or `stdout_hash` with `passed=false`; the cockpit makes no LLM review-comment call. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E022)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E023`: [E] area=`CLI/capability` anchor=`G5:L106-L109`: `elai review --cockpit [--session <id>] [--json]` reads session SSB, scores it, and prints vouched/unvouched bands plus DSR. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E023)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E024`: [E] area=`Optional integration` anchor=`G5:L106-L109`: The authored CLI optionally reads the RRA evidence manifest after G-2 ships. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E024)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E025`: [E] area=`Event schema` anchor=`G5:L111-L115`: `SsbActionType::ReviewSurfaceScored` is one Pattern-2 event with `session_id`, `dsr`, `total_changed_lines`, `attention_lines`, `vouched_hunks`, and `unvouched_hunks`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E025)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E026`: [E] area=`Authored schema evolution` anchor=`G5:L111-L115`: Authored event lifecycle is `review_surface_scored` string/parse/CHECK/round-trip/lock with schema 205→206 and variants 482→483. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E026)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E027`: [E] area=`Flag/default` anchor=`G5:L117-L119`: `ELAI_REVIEW_COCKPIT_DISABLE` is the only kill-switch; default is ON and live evidence uses no override. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E027)_

- `G-001/DG001-G5-C001`: [C] area=`Corrected identifier` anchor=`G5:L138-L140`: Dated correction says the shipped CLI function is `review_cmd.rs::run_review`, dispatched from `main.rs:489`, not a generic `run`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-C001)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-E033`: [E] area=`Dependencies` anchor=`G5:L144-L152`: Consumed evidence identifiers include TestOutcome, VibeGate/SecurityRule, GroundingSweep, ChangeDecision, FileWrite, `file_changes`, and existing review UI/CLI parser surfaces. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-E033)_
- `G-001/DG001-G5-C002`: [C] area=`Dependency correction` anchor=`G5:L153-L155`: Dated DD5 correction removes G-2 as a load-bearing shipped dependency: G-5 scores from live SSB alone via `build_file_signals` over `session_files_by_seq`; `.rra` reading is optional future enrichment. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G5-C002)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-003`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L5-L6, L31-L32`: The target metric is the gate’s marginal residual-insecure rate that reaches a human reviewer. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-003)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-005`: [E-S] anchor=`G6:L7-L10, L18-L21, L33-L38`: `SecCodePltRunner` is asserted to already compute `insecure_rate_before`, residual `insecure_rate_after`, and `gate_recall` over an embedded CWE corpus. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-005)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-006`: [E-S] anchor=`G6:L9-L11, L20-L21, L69-L74`: The existing `InsecureRateMeasured` static-gate beacon fires only inside a real `elai bench seccodeplt` invocation and is explicitly asserted never to have run live. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-006)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-007`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L11-L13, L41-L46`: G-6 generalizes that static fixed-corpus ablation into a live model/task, dynamic-exploit, anti-gaming, multi-suite matrix. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-007)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-008`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L12-L14, L46-L48`: The report headline is “fewer dynamically exploitable diffs per 100 tasks reach a reviewer,” with 95% bootstrap CI and per-CWE breakdown. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-008)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-009`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L13-L16, L47-L48, L164-L170, L207-L208`: SAFEDELTA must never be framed as a SWE-bench/model-capability rank, a “beats/#1” claim, or a perfect-code promise. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-009)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-030`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L100-L102`: `SafeDeltaReport` carries per-suite and per-CWE cells, delta/CI, and before/after dynamically exploitable counts. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-030)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-032`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L102-L103, L235-L236`: Tier 1 is the in-process static gate over embedded/held-out corpus; Tier 2 is dynamic exploit execution plus live model and competitor harnesses behind RunPod. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-032)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-033`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L105-L106`: Task checkouts remove `.git` so models cannot inspect git history or the known fix. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-033)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-034`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L105-L107`: A cheat detector flags completions that read git history or held-out answers. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-034)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-035`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L107-L108`: Train and evaluation CWEs must be disjoint held-out splits. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-035)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-036`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L107-L109, L164-L167`: A dynamic-suite diff counts insecure only when its exploit actually fires in the sandbox; static-pattern-only scoring is forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-036)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-037`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L111-L113, L168`: SAFEDELTA’s own self-test PASS is bound to F-4 `TestOutcomePayload.stdout_hash` so fabricated PASS claims are detectable. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-037)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-038`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L111-L113, L182-L183`: The report must state that a successful replay is `REPRODUCED not RIGHT`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-038)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-039`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L115-L119, L133-L139`: `safety_delta_measured` may follow the FLAT-beacon precedent or a two-arm Pattern-2 if a discriminator is needed; the choice is explicitly deferred to `G-6-decisions.md`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-039)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-040`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L118-L120`: `SafetyDeltaMeasuredPayload` fields are `suite_set`, `model_id`, `safety_delta`, `ci_low`, `ci_high`, `n_tasks`, `seed`, `corpus_blake3`, and `gate_recall`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-040)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-041`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L120-L121`: If added on the stated baseline, the event performs SQL/parse/CHECK/round-trip/golden work and bumps schema 205→206 and total variants 482→483. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-041)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-042`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L123-L127`: `elai bench safedelta` is a new bench sub-verb with its own parser, `CliAction`, command wrapper, and main dispatch, not an option on SeCodePLT. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-042)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-043`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L123-L126`: The SAFEDELTA parser accepts a suite list following `run_tier_eval` convention. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-043)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-046`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L133-L139`: The decision record must preserve ablation framing, suite/citation roles, anti-gaming controls, the CyberSecEval correction, event-shape choice, deferred claims/CI, and stale-E45 status. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-046)_

- `G-002/DG002-G6-048`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L151-L159`: Consumed identifiers are `gate_blocks`, SeCodePLT `run/from_embedded`, determinism seed/class, F-4 stdout hash, the static event precedent, bench parsers, and the future G-2 wrapper. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-048)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-049`: [E-R] anchor=`G6:L169-L170, L209-L210`: Live/dynamic/competitor arms must carry `runpod_pending` rather than an estimated or advertised live-safety result. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-049)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-050`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L171, L192-L193`: BLAKE3 is the only allowed corpus hash; BLAKE2/SHA2 are forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-050)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-051`: [E-P] anchor=`G6:L172-L173, L269-L271`: The insecure-code citation must use CyberSecEval 1/2 arXiv:2404.13161, not offensive-cyber CyberSecEval 3 arXiv:2408.01605. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-051)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-052`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L177-L179`: The Tier-1 observable is `elai bench safedelta --suites seccodeplt --seed 42` with no environment override. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-052)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-053`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L180-L181`: The live row must carry delta, CI, task count, BLAKE3 corpus digest, seed, and recall, and the delta must recompute from per-task before/after rows. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-053)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-054`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L182-L183`: The artifact must be signed/replayable once G-2 exists, and the panel/report must expose suite/CWE results, CI, and reproduced-not-right framing. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-054)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-055`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L185-L201`: Tier-1 DONE requires the wiring checker to run the static ablation and emit a real measurement; cargo-green alone is insufficient. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-055)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-056`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L202-L203, L220-L223`: The measured gate is default-on with no enable flag even though the benchmark itself is explicitly invoked. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-056)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-057`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L204-L205`: The CLI must be a real production sub-verb and the panel must pass dashboard-smoker. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-057)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-058`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L206`: The delta must be journal-recomputable under the pinned seed. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-058)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-059`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L207-L208`: Report serialization is contract-tested to contain no `rank`, `#1`, or `beats` framing. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-059)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-060`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L209-L210`: Tier-1 must be explicitly labelled static/synthetic, while pending dynamic/competitor arms preserve honest status. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-060)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-061`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L211-L212`: Anti-gaming acceptance proves `.git` removal, cheat detection, and disjoint CWE splits. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-061)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-062`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L213-L214`: SSB acceptance requires payload round-trip/action lock and the stated 205→206/482→483 bump. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-062)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-063`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L215-L217`: Completion includes workspace cargo check and auditor passes; Tier-1 adds no dependency and every Tier-2 dependency is verified and feature-gated. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-063)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-064`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L240-L243`: Static-contract cases compare against SeCodePLT before/after, recompute under seed 42, reject rank framing, and round-trip the chosen event shape. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-064)_
- `G-002/DG002-G6-065`: [E-G] anchor=`G6:L244-L247`: Anti-gaming contracts cover `.git` removal, cheat detection, disjoint splits, and the rule that an unfired dynamic exploit is not insecure. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-065)_

- `G-001/DG001-G7-E008`: [E] area=`Tiering/constraint` anchor=`G7:L17-L18`: Abstention boundary is in-process/fail-closed; authored quarantine call is local inference with no RunPod at gate time; estimated ten-to-fourteen hours. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E008)_
- `G-001/DG001-G7-E009`: [E] area=`Dependencies` anchor=`G7:L20-L24`: Required shipped dependencies are D-16 conformal surfaces, G-1 provenance/capability surfaces, provider `GGSignals`, F-4 `stdout_hash`, and the FIDES interface document. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E009)_

- `G-002/DG002-G9-002`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L5-L7, L17-L21`: Redaction runs at both the sole SSB writer and the hook-log sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-002)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-003`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L5-L10, L30-L37`: Every detected secret/PII span is transformed before persistence; post-write scrubbing is not the design. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-003)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-004`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L6-L7, L35-L37`: Replacements are stable tokens so repeated occurrences correlate without revealing the original value. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-004)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-005`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L7-L10, L32-L33`: The append-only `no UPDATE/DELETE` SSB invariant forces write-time mutation before INSERT. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-005)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-006`: [E-R] anchor=`G9:L34-L36, L95-L101`: A detector error fails closed by redacting the field instead of passing the raw value. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-006)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-007`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L10-L12, L40-L44, L74-L75`: Detection reuses F-1 `vibegate_secrets.rs::SECRET_PATTERNS`/`SECRET_LABELS`; G-9 must not define a second regex set. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-007)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-008`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L12-L15, L40-L44, L63-L64`: Presidio, ggshield, and cloud redaction services are forbidden dependencies/egress paths; the implementation is hand-rolled and local. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-008)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-009`: [E-R] anchor=`G9:L13-L15, L43-L44`: Regex/pattern detection is explicitly treated as high-recall/low-precision and its over-redaction rate is a release gate. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-009)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-010`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L17-L20, L46-L50`: The asserted SSB chain is `write_payload` → `Ssb::write` → sole `write_loop` → `execute_write`, with payload bound between `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` and `COMMIT`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-010)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-011`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L20-L21, L52-L54`: The asserted hook sink is `log_skill_invocation`, appending JSON lines to per-session `hook-executions.log`; its read side is `get_hook_executions`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-011)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-012`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L55-L58, L74-L75`: Existing secret shapes include AWS keys, typed/generic PEM headers, and secret assignments; CWE mapping detail beyond those surfaces is verify-before-cite. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-012)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-013`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L58-L60, L76`: Stable-token precedents are `source_blake3_prefix` and `blake3::hash(label)`, with workspace BLAKE3 already present. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-013)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-014`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L60-L63, L77-L78`: The database is one shared `<base>/.elai_cc/sessions/ssb.db` with `session_id` as a column, not one database per session. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-014)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-015`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L60-L63, L77-L78`: Only `hook-executions.log` is a per-session, file-granularity sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-015)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-016`: [E-S] anchor=`G9:L50-L52, L70-L71`: `run_migration` has a destructive schema-mismatch DROP, distinct from retention and ordinary append-only operation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-016)_

- `G-002/DG002-G9-041`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L163-L165`: No redaction data may leave the machine because a redactor that phones home is itself considered a leak. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-041)_

- `G-002/DG002-G9-044`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L170`: The implementation and documentation may not claim `ssb.db` is per-session. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-044)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-045`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L171, L195`: BLAKE3 is the only permitted redaction-token digest; BLAKE2/SHA2 are forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-045)_

- `G-002/DG002-G9-047`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L177-L179`: The observable fixture carries an AWS key and email through prompt/tool output, triggers hook logging, and runs with no override. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-047)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-048`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L180-L181`: The persisted row must contain `[REDACTED:aws_key:<blake3-16>]`, and searching the DB must find no raw `AKIA` key. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-048)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-049`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L182-L184`: The hook log must contain only tokens, expired hook logs must purge whole, the panel must show counts, and benign data must remain byte-identical. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-049)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-050`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L186-L203`: DONE requires wiring-checker success plus real redaction at both sinks and an expired-log purge; cargo-green alone is insufficient. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-050)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-051`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L204-L205`: Default-on acceptance proves no-override redaction and kill-switch suppression without any core ENABLE flag. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-051)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-052`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L206-L207`: Production acceptance requires both `execute_write` and `log_skill_invocation`, not test-only paths. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-052)_

- `G-002/DG002-G9-054`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L209-L210`: A database-after-commit proof must show the raw secret never landed, because later scrubbing is prohibited. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-054)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-055`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L211-L212`: Stable-token tests require identical values to map identically and benign payloads to remain unchanged. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-055)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-056`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L213-L214`: The FP gate requires zero errors on ten benign samples and a measured three-real-run rate. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-056)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-057`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L215-L216`: TTL acceptance deletes an expired hook log whole and proves no live-row UPDATE/DELETE; shared-DB retention is labelled session-scoped/whole-file. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-057)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-058`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L217-L218`: If shipped, the advisory must round-trip/golden-lock and perform the conditional 205→206/482→483 bump. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-058)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-059`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L219-L220`: Completion includes workspace cargo check/auditor and zero new dependency. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-059)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-060`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L239-L243`: The complete feature is Tier-1 in-process and genuinely fail-closed, with no network/subprocess/RunPod work. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-060)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-061`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L245-L249`: Write-time tests cover AWS key plus email, DB raw-value absence, repeated-token stability, benign identity, and detector-error fail-closed behavior. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-061)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-062`: [E-G] anchor=`G9:L250-L252`: TTL contracts cover whole-hook-log purge, no mid-session row mutation, and the chosen shared-DB retention mode. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-062)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-063`: [E-R] anchor=`G9:L287-L291`: The primary failure mode is corrupting real benign payloads by over-redaction; anchored credential-shape patterns plus measured FP gates are the mitigation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-063)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-064`: [E-R] anchor=`G9:L299-L300`: Missing any text-bearing field in any payload variant is explicitly a leak hole and requires cross-variant contract coverage. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-064)_
- `G-002/DG002-G9-065`: [E-P] anchor=`G9:L302-L306`: Once present, G-9 makes RRA/attestation export safer and establishes F-1’s detector as the single detection source for block and persistence-redaction decisions. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-065)_

### User-facing surfaces (G)

Every window, panel, drawer, command, CLI verb, flag, status display, or notification named by a SURFACE-tagged atom or surfaces inventory row. One line each with atom ID. Deduplicated by atom ID (first occurrence wins).

#### Surfaces — G-0
- `SUMMARY/G-X-002`: anchor=`GSI:L9-L12`: Each resulting capability must be dashboard-visible.
- `SUMMARY/G-X-008`: anchor=`GSI:L217-L218`: Numeric recomputation is under `ELAI_SEED`.
- `SUMMARY/G-X-012`: anchor=`GSI:L12-L14`: A session is not DONE until `wiring_contract_check.py --run-trigger` passes.

#### Surfaces — G-0 / cross-cutting
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E002`: anchor=`G0:L19-L27`: Its product surfaces are a reviewer cockpit, re-executable run artifact, and reproducible anti-gameable safety-delta measurement.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E003`: anchor=`G0:L23-L26`: The SSB SQLite-WAL append-only/no-UPDATE/no-DELETE journal plus `ELAI_SEED` is the shared credibility/replay substrate.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E007`: anchor=`G0:L49-L52`: W-1: `wiring_contract_check.py --bn-file <G-N>.md --run-trigger` must PASS, produced symbols must exist, consumers resolve to CALL/REF, BFS must reach the entrypoint, stub bans must be clean, and the live observable effect must run and emit its SSB row and artifact.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E008`: anchor=`G0:L53-L54`: W-2: no default-off environment gate may be terminal; only a disable kill-switch is allowed, default is ON, and the observable trigger runs without an override.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E009`: anchor=`G0:L55-L56`: W-3: reachability must start at a real CLI, FSM, PreToolUse/PostToolUse, or Tauri production entrypoint; a `#[cfg(test)]`-only caller is wire-dark.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E010`: anchor=`G0:L57`: W-4: any dashboard-surfaceable SSB signal requires a frontend pair and `cross_lang_diff.py` PASS.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E012`: anchor=`G0:L60`: W-6: at least one acceptance item must be verified through the dashboard using `dashboard-smoker`, or be marked N/A with one-line justification.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E019`: anchor=`G0:L75-L77`: GD-2: every emitted DSR/safety-delta/FP/confidence number must be recomputable from the SSB stream under a pinned `ELAI_SEED`; otherwise it is wire-dark.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E023`: anchor=`G0:L90-L93`: Global completion additionally requires workspace cargo check green, sole-runner auditor PASS, wiring PASS, frontend cross-language PASS where applicable, dashboard E2E, recorded GD-4 bound, and memory-writer execution.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E028`: anchor=`G0:L96-L106`: G-5 is the Wave-2, strong-grounding, medium-effort cockpit/DSR initiative.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E036`: anchor=`G0:L133`: `blast_radius_blocked` uses Kind `FsEscape/EgressDenied` and stores full pre- and post-expansion command forms.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E041`: anchor=`G0:L154-L156`: The headline composition is G-2 RRA plus G-6 SAFEDELTA: independent replayability coupled to a falsifiable published measurement.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E051`: anchor=`G0:L179-L180`: The Anthropic-MCP claim must reference the stdio command-injection family/declined protocol fix, not LiteLLM CVE-2026-30623.
- `G-001/DG001-G0-E056`: anchor=`G0:L190-L195`: A shipped session logs the pipeline `context-compiler -> integration-explorer -> spec-grounder -> executor -> auditor -> fixer(if BLOCK) -> wiring validator -> dashboard smoker(if UI) -> memory-writer -> COMMITTING`.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E018`: anchor=`G1:L96-L100`: File reads use `from_file(path)`; committed `.elai_cc/` source is Trusted and other files are Untrusted.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E022`: anchor=`G1:L104-L109`: Before git commit/push, `.elai_cc/` Write, or Bash execution, invoke `allow_tool_with_origin(tool, &tainted_context.provenance())`.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E028`: anchor=`G1:L117-L120, L171-L178`: `ELAI_TAINT_PROPAGATE_DISABLE=1` is the only kill-switch; default is ON and live evidence runs with no override; an ENABLE flag is banned.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E030`: anchor=`G1:L122-L124`: `TaintFlowDrawer.svelte`, Tauri `get_taint_flows`, and drawer key `taint-flow` list rows by Kind/trust/block state; `cross_lang_diff.py` must PASS.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E034`: anchor=`G1:L139-L140`: Produced UI identifiers are `commands_g1.rs::get_taint_flows` and `TaintFlowDrawer.svelte`.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E040`: anchor=`G1:L171-L178`: Stub bans also prohibit “wire later” literals, global join, LLM bypass, BLAKE2/SHA2, and default-off enable flags.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E041`: anchor=`G1:L182-L184`: Observable fixture injects Web/MCP text requesting `git push --force` with no env override.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E043`: anchor=`G1:L187-L188`: Expected artifact is no destructive effect, a benign Trusted flow unblocked in the same run, and drawer visibility.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E045`: anchor=`G1:L211-L227`: Acceptance requires live wiring/BFS/stub/effect PASS, default-on behavior, production `translate_tool_request`, dashboard rendering, screened-join contract, event round-trip, workspace cargo green, auditor PASS, and no new dependency.
- `G-001/DG001-G1-E049`: anchor=`G1:L262-L273`: Planned creates are safety taint module, G1 Tauri command, drawer, contract/fixtures, and decision doc; planned modifications include safety lib, SSB/golden, dashboard types/host, and a dispatch implementation.

- `G-001/DG001-G3-E036`: anchor=`G3:L170-L173`: Production reachability is `dispatch_pre_tool_use -> check_fs_target/check_egress_host -> HookFinding Block + SSB`, with Tauri-to-drawer dashboard reachability.
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E039`: anchor=`G3:L189-L192`: Evidence row must have correct Kind, both command forms, and BLAKE3 root; the file/connect side effect must not occur, benign controls remain allowed, and UI shows the diff.
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E041`: anchor=`G3:L209-L226`: Acceptance requires live wiring, default-on, production PreToolUse, dashboard E2E, populated/different command fields, pre-resolve host rejection, expanded-path escape block, FP bound, exact schema/variant bump, cargo/auditor green, and no new dependency.
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E044`: anchor=`G3:L258-L269`: Planned files create the gate module, G3 UI pair, contract/fixtures, and decision doc; modify safety lib, hook dispatcher, SSB/locks, and dashboard registration.
- `G-001/DG001-G3-E046`: anchor=`G3:L295-L296`: Non-Linux kernel confinement parity is not claimed; std::fs/host checks are the platform floor and Windows/macOS kernel-level protection remains outside scope.
- `G-001/DG001-G4-E006`: anchor=`G4:L12-L13`: Gate-time work is Tier 1, pure in-process, no RunPod/new dependency, and uses only the MCP connection ELAI already holds; estimated five-to-eight hours.
- `G-001/DG001-G4-E010`: anchor=`G4:L31-L33`: Do not attribute LiteLLM CVE-2026-30623 to Anthropic; the separate claim is the stdio command-injection family and declined protocol fix.
